AA Prototype · Requirements catch-up

The catch-up: from the RFP to the catalogue

The prototype was built in July against the original RFP, in fourteen phases. The requirements catalogue has since become the source of truth and moved a long way: Bookings instead of Cards, Slots and Draft Lists, one Contract per Procedure locked at authorise, admin-decided hospital matching, an internal ledger with Xero as its mirror. This plan closes every verified gap in 31 further phases, numbered 14 to 44, each one focused session (at most two) with one coherent deliverable, and every automatic or external event given a demo button on the screen where it matters.

Catalogue commit 1f067a8 · gap analysis 2026-09-30 · plan rendered from plan.json and gaps.json

What changed since the prototype

The prototype was built against the July 2026 RFP. The catalogue has since moved on in ways that change the core model, not only the screens. Roughly a third of the in-scope stories match, about half are partial or missing, and around 30 items contradict the catalogue outright. The contradictions cluster on four foundations: the Slot/List/Draft List split, the Contract-as-whole-pricing-decision model, the money side (AA fee, ledger, credit/rebill), and the auto-applying hospital feed. Most of EP-05, EP-09, EP-12, EP-13 and EP-14 is sound or small. EP-01, EP-02, EP-04, EP-06 and EP-08 carry most of the work.

Sizing: Contract model (DM-06, DM-09, EP-04) is XL, intake/matching (DM-28, EP-02) is XL, Slot/List split (DM-02) is XL, Booking rename plus booking-level fields (DM-01) is L, and the ledger promotion (DM-18) is L. Parent epic and feature sizes are roll-ups of their children; do not add them.

The themes below are the gap analysis's own grouping. Each lists the phases that carry its items. Full reasoning: GAP-ANALYSIS.md.

  1. Contract becomes the whole pricing decisionXL

    Replace billing route and Type 1/2/3 with one required, filtered Contract per Procedure, default hospital Contract, categories, scope filters, required inputs, invoice presentation and versions. Largest ripple across validator, invoice build, billing setup UI, review flags and seed. Size XL.

    21 items · built in phases 18, 20, 21, 22, 23, 24

  2. Contract locked at AUTHORISEDL

    Contract versions and a per-Procedure snapshot so invoices regenerate exactly and the engine stops re-resolving contract and route. Plan once across EP-04, EP-07, EP-08, EP-12 and EP-15. Size L.

    9 items · built in phases 25, 26

  3. Hospital intake becomes admin-decided matchingXL

    Staged inbound rows, matching screen, unmatched queue, field diffs, sync state and no silent apply. HL7/FHIR simulator and monitor stay demo-only (Future). Size XL.

    14 items · built in phases 33, 34

  4. Slot, List and Draft ListXL structural, then M screens

    Split the single List record into Slot, List and Draft List; availability independent of bookings; swap requests confirmed by office. Vocabulary depends on OQ-17, OQ-27, OQ-44. XL structural then M screens.

    21 items · built in phases 28, 29, 30, 31, 32

  5. Booking rename and booking-level billing fieldsL

    Card becomes Booking; billable party, invoice email, insurer/funding source, source and attachments move onto it; Copy becomes skeleton-only. Do the mechanical rename first. Size L.

    13 items · built in phases 15, 20, 21, 22

  6. Multi-procedure rule and adjustment splitM to L, pure domain

    Exactly-one primary, modifier split above four units, Contract multiProcedureRule, Contract-gated anaesthetist adjustment separate from office override. Pure domain plus capture UI. Cost FT-05.3, US-05.3.1 and US-04.2.5 once. Size M to L.

    15 items · built in phases 23, 24

  7. Prepayment derived from the anaesthetist's prepaid set

    Profile tick list, RVG groups, estimator with contingency, estimated duration, letters, credit on overpayment, trust account and refunds. Trust items Proposed; completion gate needs a user decision. Size XL epic.

    23 items · built in phases 19, 26, 27, 41

  8. Money side: AA fee, ledger, credit and rebillL each

    Remove the invented 5% fee from the payable and issue a separate AA fee invoice; promote BillingCase to linked receivable/payable records; add credit-in-full-and-rebill and admin additional invoices replacing the post-op addendum Card. Size L each.

    25 items · built in phases 16, 22, 36, 37, 39

  9. Master data and surgeon modelL

    Surgeons' rooms, surgeon profile, blacklist, hospital contact email, procedure master, RVG groups, AA codes, modifier codes, holiday master and loaders. FT-13.6 is a foundation with many consumers. Size L.

    17 items · built in phases 17, 19, 26, 32, 42

  10. Patients and admin oversightM

    Missing-NHI problem list, attach NHI, booking-time unpaid alert with threshold, patient view, admin ledger balance screens. Size M.

    13 items · built in phases 36, 40

  11. Explicit save, update email and concurrency

    Admin Booking editor moves to draft-then-save with a held change set, mailto update email, and a demo-button concurrency clash. Admin-only; anaesthetist flows keep immediate save. Size L for save.

    5 items · built in phase 35

  12. Small parity, NFR and cleanup items

    Web own-availability, NHI wording, web unbilled marker, GST period alignment, full-scale data loader, raw-message restriction, sign-in demo, plus hide/remove items from the reverse check. Size S each.

    17 items · built in phases 14, 18, 19, 29, 38, 40, 43, 44

How this works

  1. Get the owner decisions first. D1 to D11 (the table at the top of ROADMAP.md) each gate one phase. A phase whose answer has not come in builds the stated default and labels it provisional.
  2. Pick the next phase from the table below, respecting the order in "Sequencing and rules". The Contracts track (17 to 25) and the Schedule track (28 to 32) each run strictly in order.
  3. Open a fresh Claude Code session and paste that phase's kick-off prompt. The agent reads the roadmap, its phase doc, the gap detail for its items, the prototype maps and PROGRESS.md before touching anything.
  4. Drift check first. The catalogue keeps moving. Each session diffs its items against 1f067a8, reports what changed, and adapts the plan before building.
  5. The agent enters plan mode, turns the phase doc into work-sized steps, and waits for approval before changing any file.
  6. Every phase ends the same way: npm run build, npm run build:pwa, npx vitest run and (for UI work) npm run shots green; the checklist run and reported item by item; the adversarial review-and-fix pass (PROGRESS convention 18); the demo guide patched for any beat it broke; a PROGRESS.md entry. Agents never commit.
  7. When the catalogue changes, follow README.md: re-grade the changed items, merge them into gaps.json, update the affected phase docs, and re-render this page with node docs/prototype-build/catch-up/tools/build-index.mjs.

Demo buttons live on their screens. Anything that cannot be shown through normal use (scheduled jobs, backend events, external systems) gets a trigger in the harness bar's "Demo actions" menu, listed only on the screen where it matters. The installed PWA has no harness bar, so it gets the same list as a badged demo-actions sheet, plus office stand-ins for beats that wait on the office. Phase 14 builds the mechanism; every later phase registers into it.

The checklists are the contract. Each phase ends with a short manual test checklist. The kick-off prompt makes the agent run and report it, but click through it yourself too: it doubles as a rehearsal for the workshop beats that phase touches.

Sequencing and rules

Before Phase 14: ask the owner for decisions D1 to D11 (table at the top of ROADMAP.md). D1 fee netting and basis (16), D2 insurer and funding source on Booking vs Patient (20), D3 ranged base-code bound (19), D4 child payer block or warn (21), D5 prepayment completion gate (27), D6 who raises the prepayment invoice at setup (27), D7 swap without office confirmation (32), D8 ageing and Overdue (38), D9 billed Lists vanish or stay (38), D10 additional-invoice pricing (39), D11 Booking without NHI (40). Each has a default reading; a phase whose answer has not come in builds the default and labels it provisional, and its drift check confirms the gating answer first. For 27, both branches of D5 are specified: by default the gate and its override go and the escalating alert is the control; if the gate is kept, it stays but fires from the derived requirement.

Order. Run 14 and 15 first, in either order; 15 before any phase that edits booking code (if 14 runs first, 15's rename covers the registry's patterns and labels). 16 straight after both, with fee removal re-greened as step 1 before the fee invoice. The Contracts track runs strictly in order, 17 to 25: 18 keeps today's resolver; 19 (RVG and Procedure masters) comes straight after 18 so base-unit sources are final before 23's Booking-level engine and 25's lock; 20 leaves two interims (payer is the Contract holder until 21 stores a billable party; funderOverride stays the split until 22's covered amount); 25 locks what 18 to 24 built, including 24's adjustment. 26 and 27 run back to back after 25; 20's office-set prepayment flag is the interim until 27. The Schedule track runs strictly in order, 28 to 32; 31 and 32 reuse 17's blacklist helper. Intake 33 to 35 runs after 31 and 20; 35 also needs 25. 36 runs after 16, 22, 25 and 27; then 37, 38 and 39 in any order. 40 after 34 and 36; 41 after 27 and 39; 42 after every master-data phase; 43 after 42; 44 last. Contracts and Schedule are independent after 17; Contracts go first because they are mostly Confirmed and carry the S3 to S5 money beats.

Demo triggers. 14 re-homes every existing Control Panel trigger onto its screen and adds useDemoTriggerContext for state outside the URL; later phases register or re-point entries, never add to the Control Panel page, which becomes the index (scenario jumps, clock, reset). Entries declare routes, surfaces (bar, PWA, both), run(api, ctx) on the entity in the URL or the published context, and a disabled state; bodies live in src/shared or src/store for pwaPurity. PWA parity: every beat whose mobile side waits on the office or a backend event gets a PWA-only office stand-in on that mobile screen (listed per phase); 'Play the office' defaults OFF with a badge, replaced by 14's per-List 'Office authorises this List'; 44 audits parity beat by beat.

Demo guide. Each phase patches the beats it breaks in docs/demo-guide, the same sections of master-demo-guide.html and the Control Panel scenario text, in the same session. Milestone phases (16, 25, 27, 32, 35, 39) end with a consistency read of the master guide. 14 adds an interim Future-scope caveat to S1; 34 rebuilds S1; 44 rewrites S1 to S5 and regenerates the master guide in full.

Every phase opens with a drift check against 1f067a8 and closes with npm run build, npm run build:pwa, npx vitest run, and npm run shots (Playwright) for any phase touching UI, all green; the adversarial review pass (PROGRESS convention 18); PERSIST_VERSION bumped on seed changes; Decisions-log entries where a July ruling is superseded; a PROGRESS entry.

Critic points not followed: prepayment tracking with the escalating alert (US-06.3.2, Confirmed) and the re-check (US-06.3.5) stay in 27, because the alert is the control that replaces the completion gate under D5; moving it to 41 would leave S4 Beat 1 with no control for fourteen phases. Only letters, reminders and the overpaid-prepayment credit moved to 41. US-04.2.2 closes in 24, not 23: its last missing part is the Contract's allows-adjustment rule; 23 builds the base-unit override half. Phase 16 is estimated at 2 sessions rather than '1 to 2'.

The phases

ContradictsPartialMissingMatchesGroupingData model (DM)Reverse check (RV)
14

Screen-contextual demo triggers

Foundations Start here 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 1 item covered

Build one shared, pure trigger registry in src/shared/demoTriggers (its own index, modelled on demoClockShortcuts, not re-exported from the shared component barrel), plus a small useDemoTriggerContext hook for screen state the URL cannot carry, such as the Admin Integrations tab and the simulator's selected message. Each entry declares its route patterns, where it shows (harness bar, PWA sheet or both), a run(api, ctx) that acts on the entity in the URL or on the published state, a disabled reason, and an index link with an optional hint. The harness bar gets one 'Demo actions' pill that lists only the current screen's triggers and is hidden when there are none. The PWA gets the same list as a badged bottom sheet. Every Control Panel trigger moves to its own screen, except 'Run payables', whose home is the Billing monitor's existing product button. The Control Panel becomes the index, keeping scenario jumps, clock and reset. FT-13.5 gets a 'Simulate sign-in attempts' trigger on Admin Audit that writes five audit rows. 'Play the office' is off by default, badged when on, and replaced in the story by a per-List 'Office authorises this List'. The HL7/FHIR tooling gets interim Future-scope badges, and S1 gets a caveat.

FT-13.5board
Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin Audit: 'Simulate sign-in attempts' (an Admin sign-in with MFA, a failed attempt, an anaesthetist self-service password reset)
  • Admin Billing monitor: 'Trigger billing failure', 'Arm handoff failure', 'Run payables', 'Run reconciliation poll', 'Run archive job' (re-homed from the Control Panel)
  • Admin Review, on Dr Sharma's Tue 14 Jul List: 'Stage post-op scenario' (re-homed)
  • Admin Integrations, Surgeon PDFs tab (read through the context hook): 'Ingest PDF row' (re-homed)
  • Admin invoice document and Xero pair detail: 'Payment received (webhook)' Full or Half, and 'Replay last event', for the invoice in the URL (re-homed)
  • Xero sim: 'Run reconciliation poll', 'Run archive job' (re-homed)
  • Integrations simulator, Admin Integrations and mobile Lists: 'Fire hospital message' and 'Replay last (dedupe)', badged Future scope (re-homed; S1 fires it from the mobile screen)
  • PWA sheet, mobile List: 'Office authorises this List' (so 'Play the office' can default OFF, badged when on)
  • PWA sheet, mobile Balances: 'Payment received' Full or Half on an open invoice

Risks. pwaPurity.test forbids shell or demo imports in the PWA closure, so the registry, the context hook, trigger bodies and shared OFFICE and SOUTER actor constants live in src/shared or src/store. The 48px bar has no responsive handling: one menu pill, not many buttons. Office stand-ins declare the PWA surface only, because in the framed build the presenter plays the office in Admin. Playwright data-shot hooks (control-payment-webhook, control-scheduled-jobs, control-integration-message) move with their triggers, so the specs change here. Re-home first and re-green, then add the new pieces.

What to check when it's done
  • The harness bar shows no 'Demo actions' pill on the Admin Day view. On the Billing monitor it lists exactly four entries. Every Control Panel 'Open screen' link, with its hint where shown, lands on a screen whose menu contains that entry, and no trigger buttons remain on the Control Panel.
  • Each re-homed trigger behaves exactly as its Control Panel card did: same store calls, actors, guards and idempotency keys, and half-payment falls back to the full amount when half rounds to 0. 'Ingest PDF row' appears only on the Surgeon PDFs tab. Replay after a reset refuses cleanly.
  • Installed PWA with fresh or corrupt storage: 'Play the office' is OFF and a submitted List waits. The Demo chip on that List runs 'Office authorises this List', which authorises and bills it. Balances takes a chosen payment. pwaPurity.test stays green, with the bar menu forbidden.
  • 'Simulate sign-in attempts' adds five labelled audit rows through mutate() only: provisioning, Admin sign-in with MFA, failed sign-in, self-service reset and mobile social login. Future-scope badges show on the simulator and on the Messages and Feed config tabs. The S1 caveat is in place. build, build:pwa, vitest, shots and verify:board are all green, and the 4 specs, 18 recipes and ATLAS are updated.
Kick-off prompt - paste into a fresh Claude Code session 
Please run catch-up Phase 14 (Screen-contextual demo triggers) of the Anaesthesia Associates prototype.

Repo root: /Users/d.fraser/Local Dev/Anaesthesia Associates RFP (the folder name has spaces, so quote paths). The app is aa-prototype/; every path below is relative to the repo root. Follow CLAUDE.md. Do not commit or push.

Before doing anything else, read these in order:
1. docs/prototype-build/catch-up/ROADMAP.md - the sequencing rules and the "Demo triggers", "PWA parity" and "Demo guide" sections (this phase builds the mechanism every later phase registers into).
2. docs/prototype-build/catch-up/phases/phase-14-demo-trigger-registry.md - your detailed plan.
3. docs/prototype-build/catch-up/GAP-ANALYSIS.md - "Demo-trigger buttons", "Remove or rework" and "Demo impact (S1 to S5)"; then docs/prototype-build/catch-up/epics/EP-13.md (FT-13.5) and docs/prototype-build/catch-up/analysis/reverse-check.md (RV-05, RV-06, RV-22).
4. The catalogue items: "docs/discovery-reference/Updated Requirements/catalogue/requirements/FT-13.5.md", US-13.5.1.md and US-13.5.2.md in the same folder.
5. docs/prototype-build/catch-up/analysis/prototype-map-shell-demo-pwa.md (all of it: every Control Panel trigger, the PWA panel and office simulation, the extension points) and the Audit, Billing monitor, Invoices and Integrations sections of prototype-map-admin.md.
6. docs/design/Design Language.dc.html (tokens, badge tints, sheet-in motion) and docs/design/Mobile App.dc.html (the bottom-sheet pattern, and the header and dock positions the PWA chip must clear). The harness bar has no mockup: copy aa-prototype/src/shell/DemoClockMenu.tsx.
7. docs/prototype-build/PROGRESS.md - binding conventions (especially 4, 12, 13, 16, 17, 18) and the Decisions log entries "2026-07-28 Demo clock promoted into the global harness" and "2026-09-28 Anaesthetist Card shows no calculation" (the precedent for harness-bar and PWA-panel controls), plus the Phase 12 entry (how the Control Panel was finished). Then aa-prototype/README.md's Office simulation section and the "Demo control panel" section of requirements-board/capture/ATLAS.md.

Then do the drift check in the phase doc (git diff 1f067a8 for FT-13.5, its two stories, US-07.2.2, US-07.3.1 and FT-14.1 to 14.6), and report what changed. Enter plan mode: confirm the registry contract (types, surfaces, when, choices, disabledReason, indexPath, indexHint, memory, context keys), map each Control Panel handler to its registry entry (and say which one, "Run payables", is dropped because the Billing monitor's product button is its home), split the work into the two sessions the doc describes, and wait for my approval. Do not edit any file before I approve.

While working:
- Re-home first, then re-green, then add the new pieces. Every re-homed body does exactly what its Control Panel card did (same store calls, actors, guards and idempotency keys); only where it shows and how it finds its target change.
- The registry, useDemoTriggerContext, the replay memory, the shared OFFICE_ACTOR / SOUTER_ACTOR / OFFICE_SIMULATION_ACTOR constants and the office stand-in live in src/shared or src/store. The registry has its own index under src/shared/demoTriggers and is not re-exported from the src/shared/index.ts component barrel. Nothing in the PWA closure may import src/apps/*, src/shell/AppShell.tsx or the bar menu; pwaPurity.test.ts must stay green.
- A trigger acts on the entity in the URL or on published screen state wherever it can; a seed-scoped entry names its seed constant and is gated with when. No Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random().
- One "Demo actions" pill in the 48px bar, hidden on screens with no entries; never a row of buttons. Harness chrome is white-on-ink with amber demo badges; teal only for Run; crimson never. Every trigger is badged (convention 13).
- Office stand-ins declare the PWA surface only. "Play the office" defaults OFF (fresh or corrupt storage reads OFF) and is badged when on. The PWA sheet is a bottom sheet with tappable choices, not a dropdown (convention 16).

The fee basis sits in one labelled constant marked as a demo assumption (D1, OQ-02 open). Admin and a new web Accounts 'AA fees' tab show each fee invoice paid or unpaid, with screen-scoped triggers for the scheduled run and the fee payment. Both Xero records store InvoiceNumber (with -P on the ACCPAY) and Reference.

Surgeons, rooms and blacklist

Master data After phases 14, 15 1 session plan doc ↗ 5 items covered

Make surgeons real master data. Surgeon becomes a profile with an NZ medical registration number, one HPI identifier labelled "HPI number (CPN)" (provisional until OQ-52 answers) and a required link to a surgeons' room. Rooms get a contact email and phone, and their members are worked out from each surgeon's room link rather than stored. Surgeon groups get member surgeons, so Phase 18 has a Surgeon Group holder. Every hospital gets a contact email. An audited blacklist of anaesthetist and surgeon pairings is kept on the surgeon profile. Ending an entry keeps its history, and re-adding the pairing creates a new entry. Admin gets editors for these masters, a Master data view held in the URL (?view=) and a surgeon profile page at /admin/masters/surgeons/:surgeonId. The first use is in the office's assignment pickers (Edit list, phone advice, Reassign, Permanent list). There, blacklisted options sit in their own labelled group and stay selectable. Choosing one shows a soft amber warning naming both people, and going ahead writes one *.blacklistAcknowledged audit entry, for office actors only. The rule lives in the pure src/domain/blacklist.ts, and SurgeonSelect and BlacklistWarning live in src/shared/schedule, so Phases 28, 31 and 32 reuse them. Admin only (OQ-43). 18

Contract model

Contracts After phase 17 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 9 items covered

Contract changes from Type 1/2/3 to four parts. (1) Category: the six catalogue categories, no Pre-paid, including a protected RVG Default Post-paid and a split between Surgeon Solo and Surgeon Group. (2) Holder: hospital, surgeon, surgeon group or the Booking's billable party. (3) Scope filters on hospitals, surgeons, insurers, RVG codes, funding source and a set of anaesthetists. (4) Pricing basis. Contracts get a review date and an explicit retire. ContractPrice becomes FeeScheduleLine: holder code and description, ex and inc GST, time band, add-on, quantity rule, optional RVG mapping, and effective-dated prices shown as 'Upcoming from <date>'. Contract rates also get effective dates. fee.ts reprices from the new shape. A parity fixture captured before the reshape proves every seeded fee and counterparty is unchanged. COS still bills as its organisation, through the Phase 17 surgeon group. Today's resolver is kept. ACC loses its special-case markers and is priced as an ordinary holder. The Admin Contract catalogue and detail panel are rebuilt, and office billing setup can pick a schedule line and add-ons.

  • Admin, Master data, Contracts: every Contract shows category, holder, pricing and scope, and none shows 'Type n'. The six defaults (five hospital, nib) plus RVG Default Post-paid show a lock. Health NZ shows 'Review due'. Retire works and hides the Contract unless 'Show retired' is on
  • Doyle BAR-BYP shows $2,800 current and 'Upcoming from 28 Jul 2026 · $2,950'. After '+7 days' it flips to current, but the Tue 14 Jul bariatric Booking still prices $2,800 and $950. The CES HNZ schedule prices a 75-minute vitrectomy at HNZVIT90, plus add-ons
  • No ACC chip, column, review flag or accRelated anywhere, but the ACC pre-op help text stays. 'Trigger billing failure' and the monitor's restore still work on the COS Contract. PERSIST_VERSION is bumped. build, build:pwa, vitest and shots are all green

19

RVG, modifier and Procedure masters

Contracts After phase 18 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 8 items covered

Settles where base units come from before the engine (23) and the lock (25) build on them. The RVG master becomes editable: admins can add AA-sourced codes, and NZSA guide values stay read-only. AA-defined RVG groups are many-to-many tags on codes, alongside site groups derived from the NZSA sections. They feed Phase 26's prepaid tick list and a new Contract scope field that Phase 20 filters by. The modifier master becomes store data in the catalogue set, with editable units and a selection rule on each code: VM1, TTE1/2, PACU1, EAA1, POC1 to 3 and NC1/2 are added and PO1/PO2 removed. An office-maintained Procedure master maps operation names to an RVG code or group, each with its own base units. One pure resolver sets the base-unit precedence: manual override, then a Contract override (a slot that 23 fills), then the Procedure master, then the RVG guide value. Under D3, ranged base units are no longer bounded at completion, and an out-of-range value raises an office review flag. ACC pre-op codes CS250, CS260 and CS70 become coded fixed lines, offered only on Contracts whose funding-source scope includes ACC. The capture picker does not change (OQ-06), and a fee parity fixture proves that no S1 to S5 figure moves.

One Contract per Procedure

Contracts After phase 19 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 13 items covered

Pricing becomes one decision. BillingRoute, PatientPaymentCategory and Procedure.insurerId are removed, along with the engine's route resolution and route-based payer. Every Procedure carries exactly one Contract. It defaults to the List hospital's RVG Default Hospital Contract (RVG Default Post-paid when the List has no hospital) on every creation path, and again when the hospital changes. The anaesthetist (mobile and web, DRAFT only) and the office choose it from one pure, store-enforced picker filtered by hospital, surgeon, insurer, funding source, RVG code or group, and anaesthetist. The default is always offered, there is no "None", and an insurer-held Contract is not offered while that insurer takes no direct claims. Insurer and funding source move onto the Booking (D2). An anaesthetist's change is recorded and flagged in Admin review. Three labelled interims hold until later phases: the payer is the Contract holder (21), funderOverride keeps the Prentice split (22), and pre-payment is an office-set Booking flag (27). Every S3 to S5 figure and seeded payer stays identical.

21

Billable party, required inputs and completeness

Contracts After phase 20 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 12 items covered

Each Booking gets a billable party, meaning who receives the invoice. It defaults from each Procedure's Contract holder, or from the patient on the patient-direct RVG Default Post-paid Contract. The office or the anaesthetist can override it without changing the price, for example to a guardian, a hospital or another organisation. The billing run then raises one invoice per distinct party, which replaces Phase 20's interim rule that the payer is the Contract holder. A patient-direct Booking needs an invoice email, defaulted from the patient's or guardian's own and snapshotted on the invoice. Contracts declare the inputs each Booking must hold: invoice email, billable party, prepaid amount, member number, claim reference and purchase order. Mark complete needs a Contract on every Procedure, times on every Procedure and every declared input. A fixed-schedule Contract with no line for the code no longer prices silently by BTM. The Procedure is flagged 'to confirm with the hospital', only the office can set that Contract, and the office resolves it either with default RVG pricing and an explicit choice of who is invoiced, or by adding a new schedule line. Office Review shows every Procedure's Contract with Approve or Correct, plus who is invoiced with their email and address, the insurer line, and neutral flags for no Contract or an insurer still to add. It also flags a child billable party. Under D4's provisional default, a child payer or an open schedule miss blocks authorising the List. These are the only two blockers, and both come from one pure function.

Invoice presentation and delivery

Billing After phase 21 1 session plan doc ↗ 8 items covered

Every procedure invoice is issued in the anaesthetist's name, with their GST number, and names AA as agent. This wording is provisional until OQ-29 is answered. Each Contract has its own invoicing settings, using the domain model's names: invoiceLayout (contract holder or patient), deliveryMethod (email, portal or none) and gstTreatment (prices are still stored GST exclusive). The billing run sends every invoice itself, through one shared materialiser used by the run, the retry and the prepayment raise. It stamps each invoice 'Emailed to <address>' (the Booking's invoice email, which can be a guardian's) or 'Queued for portal'. The office keeps only Resend or Send on the invoice rail. A Contract's covered amount or percent (proposed, OQ-23) replaces funderOverride: the holder is invoiced for the covered part and the billable party for the gap. S3 keeps its figures ($396.18, $152.38, $91.43). The Xero ACCPAY carries a snapshot of provisional buyer-created tax invoice wording. AA fee invoices (Phase 16) are exempt. No new harness trigger: the sends show on the existing Admin Review authorise, and on the PWA's 'Office authorises this List' and 'Play the office'.

24

Contract-gated anaesthetist adjustment

Contracts After phase 23 1 session plan doc ↗ 7 items covered

The anaesthetist adjustment becomes its own record, separate from the office override (today's priceOverride, renamed officeOverride). It is a percent discount (more than 0, up to 100) or a fixed final price. There is no dollar kind, and a reason is always required. It is offered only when the Procedure's Contract allows it (the new allowsAnaesthetistAdjustment rule, edited in the Contract detail panel) and only once B, T and M are recorded. Since Phase 23, B and M are read from the Booking's primary. B, T and M stay recorded even at a 100% discount. The pure engine prices the Booking-level base, then the adjustment, then the office override. The office override stays available on every Contract, including over a fixed fee. On mobile and web the field is absent where the Contract does not allow it, and no fee is shown. Review, the admin Booking total and the invoice show both layers. This closes US-04.2.2's pricing and adjustment rules.

DM-12RV-11FT-03.5boardUS-03.5.1boardFT-05.4boardUS-05.4.1boardUS-04.2.2board

Open questions: OQ-06

Demo triggers and risks

No new demo triggers: this phase demos through normal use.

Risks. Seeded dollar adjustments must be re-expressed as percent or fixed final, or moved to office overrides; check S5's audited edits. US-03.5.1 and US-04.2.2 are Proposed. The pure engine order gets worked-example tests (adjustment then override, over a fixed-fee Contract).

What to check when it's done
  • Where the Contract does not allow it (the Aria rate x time Booking, any St George's default Booking), mobile and web show no Price adjustment section at all. On the RVG Default Post-paid Booking it appears live once B, T and the primary's ASA are recorded, needs a reason, and still shows no fee.
  • Worked examples A to H and G2 pass in adjustments.test.ts and the engine tests: base, then adjustment, then office override, rounded to cents. A 100% discount leaves B, T, M and billableUnits intact. The office override works over a fixed-fee Contract.
  • Store gates hold. setAnaesthetistAdjustment refuses a disallowed Contract, unrecorded BTM or a blank reason. The office can only clear a disallowed adjustment. The anaesthetist cannot write officeOverride. editProcedure refuses both fields. A Contract change clears a disallowed adjustment after a warning.
  • Review on Dr Morrison's Mon 20 Jul List shows the discounted Booking with "was $x" and the discount flag. The office override sheet shows both context lines. The invoice carries both delta lines and reconciles to the cent. S3 figures are unchanged, PERSIST_VERSION is bumped, and build, build:pwa, vitest and shots are green.

25

Contract versions and the AUTHORISED lock

Contracts After phases 19, 22 to 24 1 session plan doc ↗ 8 items covered

A Contract stops being one mutable record. Every committed change to a Contract or its fee schedule adds an immutable version in the same mutate() commit, and the Contract editor lists those versions with the invoices raised under each. When the office authorises a List, authoriseList writes a locked record for every Procedure in the same commit that flips the List to AUTHORISED. The record holds the Contract id and a full copy of its version, the pricing date and rate, the base units and where they came from, time units, modifiers, the schedule line and add-ons, the primary flag, the multi-procedure rule and this Procedure's share of the split, and the anaesthetist adjustment and office override. At Booking level it also holds the party details the invoice prints, the GST rate, required inputs, the covered amount, presentation and any pre-payment. The billing run, retry and regenerate price only from that record: there is no effective-date fallback, no route or payer resolution, and no "Resolve & retry" that edits the master. A Contract that is not in force becomes a Review authorise blocker. Locked Bookings display from the snapshot, with a "Contract vN locked at authorise" badge. A harness-bar "Regenerate from locked data" button on the Admin invoice proves the rebuild is identical. The billing-failure trigger is re-based on a missing claim reference, which the office supplies and retries once.

DM-07RV-01US-04.1.3boardUS-04.3.5boardUS-07.3.1boardUS-08.1.1boardUS-08.4.4boardUS-15.0.3board
Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin invoice document: 'Regenerate from locked data' (identical output, including after a Contract or unit-value edit)
  • Admin Billing monitor: re-homed 'Trigger billing failure', re-pointed to a cause that survives the lock (a missing required reference or a handoff fault)
  • Existing Master data unit-value edit and the S5 Beat 4 contract edit now demonstrate the lock

Risks. Which fields to freeze is still a discovery item, so snapshot the whole Contract version plus the rate inputs. S4 Beat 3 and S5 Beat 4 are re-scripted here. Retry must stay idempotent and must not duplicate invoices. Seeded AUTHORISED Lists need locks written at seed time.

What to check when it's done
  • S3 and S5 invoices match Phase 24 to the cent (the phase-25 parity fixture is never updated with -u). Each authorised Booking shows the neutral 'Contract vN locked at authorise' pill in all three apps, and Review shows the lock glyph with the version and '(locked)' on the rate.
  • After authorising, edit Dr Souter's unit value and end-date Health NZ. Locked Bookings, Review and 'Regenerate from locked data' still show identical figures. A DRAFT Booking re-prices. The Contract's Version history shows v2 with 0 invoices and v1 with Whitaker's invoices. A still-unlocked List is blocked by contractNotInForce, with no silent default.
  • Billing monitor 'Trigger billing failure': Tuilagi fails requiredInputMissing and Walker bills. 'Add and retry' raises exactly one invoice and one Xero pair, a second retry is refused, and no Contract version is added. contractIneffective and defaultContractFor grep to zero.
  • npm run build, build:pwa, npx vitest run and npm run shots are green (plus verify:board after the recipe edits). PERSIST_VERSION is bumped once, every seeded AUTHORISED List (including the migrated L-HIST ones) is fully locked, and the demo guide S3, S4 Beat 3 and S5 Beat 4 plus the master-guide consistency read are done.

27

Prepayment lifecycle

Prepayment After phases 25, 26 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 12 items covered

Prepayment stops being picked by a person. It is worked out from the Booking's Procedures against the anaesthetist's prepaid set (Phase 26), using expandPrepaidCodes, and this replaces Phase 20's interim flag. A pure estimator with Vitest tests adds base units from resolveBaseUnits, time units from an estimated duration the office enters, and contingency units held as a setting, all at the anaesthetist's own unit value. A prepaid additional Procedure is estimated the way Phase 23 will charge it. The estimate or a deposit is stored on the Booking and can be set by the office or the anaesthetist. The prepayment invoice is raised at setup, per owner decision D6. The office tracks each prepaid Booking as unpaid, part paid or paid, with an alert that escalates as the procedure date approaches. Procedure, Contract, duration and anaesthetist changes trigger a re-check. After AUTHORISED, a balance invoice names the prepayment it settles. If the prepayment was more than the final fee, the difference is recorded as an interim labelled excess and no negative invoice is raised. Owner decision D5 fixes the completion gate: by default the gate and its override are removed and the alert is the control.

Open questions: OQ-38 · OQ-50

This builds on billing cases that Phase 36 promotes. Letters, reminders, overpayment credit and refunds are Phase 41.

28

Slot and List split

Schedule After phases 14, 15 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 7 items covered

Split the canvas into Slots and Lists. Every anaesthetist gets an AM and a PM Slot each day, with a fixed id. The Slot holds its availability, a note and optional start and end times. A List becomes its own record with a slotId and a kind (private, public, pre-op). It is created only when the office assigns one (a surgeon and a hospital are required) or when a Permanent List projects one. One pure displayStatusKey(slot, list) gives the status in mobile, web and admin, reads no Bookings, and keeps the six colours. Default AM and PM times come from one settings record, and a Slot can override them. Reassign moves a List between Slots and keeps its id, Bookings and history; the vacated Slot goes back to available by default. The generator, roll-forward, selectors, Admin Day grid and drawer, mobile and web schedule screens, the Phase 14 registry and the demo inspector all read the new records. A golden fixture proves the seed is reproduced exactly. The DRAFT label now reads "Open", and PERSIST_VERSION is bumped.

DM-02DM-04RV-14FT-01.2boardUS-01.2.3boardUS-01.3.3boardUS-01.1.4board

Open questions: OQ-17 · OQ-27

Demo triggers and risks
  • PWA sheet, mobile Lists: 'Office assigns a List to my next free Slot'

Risks. The largest structural change on the schedule side: listForSlot and bookingsForList have callers everywhere, including re-homed triggers. Keep the six-colour design language by deriving the display key. The status vocabulary is a labelled simplification until OQ-17 and OQ-27 are answered. Re-walk S2 Beats 1 to 3; a vacated Slot now defaults to available.

What to check when it's done
  • Golden canvas test reproduces today's statuses, times, notes, conflicts and every seeded Booking. Seed invariants hold: two Slots per anaesthetist per day, every List in exactly one Slot with matching copied (denormalised) fields, every Booking's listId resolves. Roll-forward and addAnaesthetist deep-equal a fresh generation from SEED_LEAVE
  • After Assign List or Book (phone advice) in Admin, the session shows as the same booked List in the Admin grid, mobile Forward Lists and Availability, and web week strip, Lists and Availability grid. Adding, completing or cancelling Bookings never changes its colour or label
  • Reassign keeps the List id, Bookings and history, writes one list.reassign and no absorb or regenerate entry, and leaves the vacated Slot Available unless the office marks it otherwise. Changing the default session times moves every Slot that has no override. No 'DRAFT' shows on an empty Slot or an assigned List
  • PWA 'Office assigns a List to my next free Slot' shows only on Mobile Lists and is badged office stand-in. Nothing new appears in the harness bar. npm run build, build:pwa, vitest and shots are green; PERSIST_VERSION is bumped; the S2 Beats 1 to 3 and S4 Beat 2 demo-guide patches are in place

29

Availability calendar and status master

Schedule After phase 28 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 5 items covered

Anaesthetists set half-day availability weeks ahead from a month calendar on mobile (a slide-in layer with a bottom sheet) and on web (a desktop page with a right-rail form and range selection). Choices are available, available for emergency, unavailable or on leave, for one Slot, a whole day or a date range. Both apps use one shared form and one store action, which closes the web parity gap. A bookable status on a booked Slot no longer raises a false conflict and clears an earlier one. A closed status flags the List and never changes it. The Slot status set becomes editable master data: admins add, rename, recolour (from the six design tokens only) and retire statuses. A status key is the value stored on Slot.availability or List.kind, and every app reads the records. Everything is labelled provisional under OQ-17 and OQ-27.

US-01.2.1boardUS-01.2.2boardUS-01.5.3boardUS-03.1.4boardUS-15.0.2board

The theme must read status records instead of a hardcoded union without breaking the design language. The web app stays desktop-width.

What to check when it's done
  • Mobile My calendar and web My availability both set a leave range through the same form. The Admin Day grid shows it at once, and a booked List in that range gets one amber conflict with its surgeon, hospital and Bookings unchanged. Setting it back to Available clears the conflict.
  • Admin Slot statuses: rename Unavailable, recolour it and add 'Sick leave'. Every chip, legend and grid in all three apps follows with no reload. Retiring the default, a status a Permanent List uses, or a label containing an em dash is refused.
  • The seeded emergency pair (for example Hughes Tue 28 Jul) is the only diff in Phase 28's golden canvas fixture. It draws as a solid green 'Emergency' block and is left out of Free only and cover. No new hex, no crimson control, and the seeded statuses keep today's hatch and dashed strings.
  • npm run build, npm run build:pwa, npx vitest run (with domainPurity and pwaPurity) and npm run shots are green. S2 runs unchanged apart from the 'On leave' label. The demo guide patches match master-demo-guide.html.

Schedule After phase 29 1 session plan doc ↗ 5 items covered

Make a conflict one derived rule. A List conflicts when its hospital is closed that day or its Slot's status is closed (Phase 29's isClosed). mutate() re-checks every List a change touches, so every path raises a flag: assign, phone advice, edit, move, availability, holidays, templates and roll-forward. The warning stays soft (OQ-09): nothing refuses, Bookings stay, and the List turns the design's amber warning tint, keeping its kind bar and label. A new Admin Conflicts screen lists every conflict across dates with reason, anaesthetist, hospital, Slot status and Booking count, with Reassign, Clear and Open on each row. Permanent List add, edit, end and remove change the canvas from tomorrow and never touch Lists with Bookings or office edits. Weekend templates and end dates work. "Simulate sickness" sits in the Day view and Conflicts Demo actions, and a PWA-only "Office reassigns this List" stand-in covers the handset.

Draft Lists and the day dashboard

Schedule After phases 17, 30 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 9 items covered

Add the Draft List. It records a hospital, an optional surgeon (under the OQ-44 interim), a day, a session, a note, where it came from and when it was created. It has no anaesthetist, takes no Slot and holds no Bookings. The office creates Draft Lists and sees them flagged "Being prepared", with how long each has waited, in three places: a Draft Lists view, a band on the Day dashboard, and an amber nav badge. A Draft List at a hospital closed that day shows a warning that is not a conflict. The office assigns it to an anaesthetist's Slot for that day and session. An occupied Slot is refused. Unavailable, emergency-only, blacklisted and hospital-holiday choices warn and still go ahead, and all of it is audited. The pairing rule (exactly one surgeon and one hospital) is enforced on every path, so List.hospitalId and surgeonId become required. In the seed, the Fitzgerald "Surgeon TBC" List becomes a Draft List, pre-op Lists sit at a new AA rooms location, and every public and pre-op List names a surgeon. No fee moves. Each Day dashboard block also shows its booking count.

32

Swap requests

Schedule After phases 17, 29 1 session plan doc ↗ 4 items covered

Replace the cover-request marker (RV-15) with a SwapRequest (DM-05). From the Find cover availability view on mobile and web, an anaesthetist asks for one of her own upcoming Lists to go to a named colleague. The colleagues offered follow the same receive rule as Phase 28's moveListToSlot. If the colleague is blacklisted with the List's surgeon, she sees a short prompt worded per the OQ-43 recommendation (no reason, no surgeon name, no "blacklist" word) and can still send. The office confirms or declines in a new Admin Swap requests queue (D7 default). Confirming moves the List between Slots through the one moveListToSlot core, in a single audited commit, and shows the office the full BlacklistWarning. Both anaesthetists are notified through a simulated Swaps panel, and the requester can withdraw. Triggers: 'Colleague requests a swap' in the Admin harness bar, plus PWA-only office stand-ins to confirm or decline.

DM-05RV-15US-01.4.3boardUS-01.4.5board

Open questions: OQ-39 · OQ-43

Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin swap queue: 'Colleague requests a swap' (stages a pending request, so the office side shows without switching persona)
  • PWA sheet, mobile availability: 'Office confirms this swap' and 'Office declines this swap' on a pending request

Risks. If D7 comes back 'direct handover', the confirm step becomes an office notification and the queue becomes a log; keep the transition in one store action so the switch is small.

What to check when it's done
  • Nothing moves until the office confirms. confirmSwap uses the same moveListToSlot core as Reassign list, in one commit that writes a single list.reassign carrying swapRequestId. A request overtaken by events (reassigned, cancelled, submitted, day passed, target Slot filled) shows its reason in the queue, cannot be confirmed, and can still be declined.
  • The mobile app, web app and PWA only ever get the OQ-43 prompt. No blacklist reason, surgeon name, entry id or the word 'blacklist' appears, and the blacklist never blocks. The Admin confirm sheet shows the full warning with its reason and 'Confirm swap anyway'.
  • RV-15 is fully gone: no coverRequest, requestCover, RequestCoverSheet, offerCover, CoverTarget/onCover, 'Offer cover', 'Cover requested' or 'Tap to ask' in src, visual or the capture recipes. The kept 'open for cover' free-Slot notes still work for the Admin Day recipes.
  • From a reset, the swap queue is empty and S2 Beats 1 to 4 run unchanged, with Sharma's Wed 22 AM still open. The Admin trigger shows only on /admin/swaps, and the stand-ins only in the PWA sheet. PERSIST_VERSION is bumped. build, build:pwa, vitest, shots and verify:board are green, and the demo guide (S2 Beat 3b, Workflow 3, personas, cheat sheet, master guide) has been patched and read through for consistency.

Hospital download and the matching screen

Intake After phases 20, 31 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 11 items covered

Hospital data stops going straight onto the schedule. A new Admin Matching screen, laid out like Admin Review, takes hospital downloads and sheets as rows. The first is a labelled sample file from St George's, Southern Cross or a Forte manual sheet, via 'Import hospital download', while OQ-13 is open. HL7/FHIR messages (badged Future scope) also arrive as rows. Each row shows its change type in plain words (New, Modification, Reschedule, Cancellation, No change) and the field changes side by side. The admin matches and applies it, creates a Booking, a List in a Slot or a Draft List, or rejects it with a reason. Rows with no match wait in a derived unmatched queue: a reschedule to a date with no List, an unknown appointment, or a closed Draft List. Nothing reaches the schedule until the admin decides. Changes go through the existing guarded actions as the office. New Bookings take the default Contract and source 'hospital download'. A note is added, never overwritten. A field the hospital left out is never a change. An NHI or Contract is never set from a row. The bar gets two demo actions on Matching. The PWA gets an office stand-in so S1 still runs on a handset.

Explicit save and the update email

Intake After phases 17, 25, 33 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 6 items covered

Admin Booking editing becomes draft-then-save: the office edits freely, sees an unsaved-changes bar with Save changes and Discard, and gets a warning on leaving (side nav, app switcher, browser back, reload). Each save writes one change set in the append-only audit, including every Contract selection and adjustment. History shows each save as one row, with a read-only 'as at' view of the Booking at any point. Every Booking and Procedure carries a row version. If someone else saved a different field in the meantime, the two merge; if they changed the same field, a plain clash prompt asks which to keep; a cancel, move or authorise in between blocks the save. After a saved change or a List reassignment, 'Draft update email' opens a real mailto (to the surgeon's rooms or the hospital, labelled provisional under OQ-46), listing only allowlisted changes and shortened with a note past 2,000 characters, beside a badged preview panel. The office can add a Booking to an already booked List and type a time. Anaesthetist flows on mobile, web and the PWA keep immediate saves. The triggers are 'Someone else saves this Booking now' (Admin Booking detail, harness bar) and 'Office edits this Booking' (PWA mobile Booking).

DM-29US-02.3.1boardUS-02.3.2boardUS-02.3.3boardUS-02.5.5boardUS-02.5.6board

Open questions: OQ-46

Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin Booking detail while editing: 'Someone else saves this Booking now' (reads the edit draft through the context hook; a different-field merge, or a same-field clash on Save)
  • Admin Booking detail: 'Draft update email' preview panel alongside the real mailto (product)
  • PWA sheet, mobile Booking: 'Office edits this Booking' (a saved office change arrives on the handset)

Risks. Because nothing applies silently, the concurrency trigger simulates a second office user, not the hospital feed. The shared Booking detail must keep the mobile and web immediate-save paths intact. S5 Beat 1 audit gains change sets.

What to check when it's done
  • On the Admin Booking detail, three edits show '3 unsaved changes'. Commands are disabled until you save or discard, sheets say Apply, and leaving by side nav, app switcher, back button or reload warns. Save writes one change set (one id, a booking.save header, the office as actor, rowVersion up by one). History shows it as one row, including any Contract or adjustment change, and View as at rebuilds the earlier state with no money. Demo Reset discards a dirty draft.
  • Web and mobile Booking edits still save immediately: no bar, no Apply wording, and the anaesthetist never calls saveBookingChanges. Firing 'Someone else saves this Booking now' on a dirty draft, then pressing Save, merges a different field and says so, or opens the clash sheet for the same field. The PWA's 'Office edits this Booking' shows the office banner and a grouped office save.
  • Draft update email is a real mailto link: it goes to the rooms after a Booking save, to the hospital after a reassignment, and leaves To empty when no contact is held. Only allowlisted fields appear (no NHI, DOB, notes, Contract or money), the link is at most 2,000 characters with a shortened note when it had to cut, and opening it adds no audit entry. Add booking works on a List that already has Bookings, and the S2 phone-advice prefill still works.
  • npm run build, npm run build:pwa, npx vitest run and npm run shots are green, with PERSIST_VERSION bumped and pwaPurity and the registry tests passing. The demo guide has S5 Beat 1, S2 Beat 3, Save changes steps, the cheat sheet's Concurrency topic and the master guide's concurrency callouts updated, and no 'last-write-wins' or 'single-user' is left.

36

Internal ledger and balance views

Money After phases 16, 22, 25, 27 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 9 items covered

BillingCase becomes a ledger pair, and the ledger becomes AA's system of record. Every invoice creator (the billing run, the retry, the prepayment raise, the AA fee run) creates the pair in the same mutate(), before and independent of the Xero handoff. A pair has a receivable leg and a payable leg, each with its own number (the invoice number, and the same number with -P), amount, dated receipts or disbursements and Xero mirror id, and links to the Booking, the Procedures and the patient's hidden id. AA fee invoices become receivable-only pairs. Payments, payables due and the payables run read the ledger first and mirror to Xero. A billing-run failure becomes a BillingException. A receipt that matches nothing is held until the office allocates or refunds it. Pure positions are derived for the whole ledger (in, out, imbalance), each anaesthetist (owed, collected, paid out, what they owe AA) and each patient. A new Admin Ledger screen shows the whole ledger and one anaesthetist, with an in-balance indicator. Web Accounts, mobile Balances, the monitor, the invoice document and the Xero sim all re-point to the ledger. A parity test keeps every presenter figure unchanged.

DM-18DM-19EP-08boardFT-08.3boardUS-08.3.1boardUS-08.3.2boardUS-08.3.4boardUS-08.3.5boardUS-13.2.1board
Demo triggers and risks
  • Admin Ledger: 'Inject unmatched receipt' (a Xero payment with no matching receivable, so the imbalance goes non-zero)
  • Re-homed 'Payment received (webhook)' and Billing monitor 'Run payables'
  • Optional, Admin Billing monitor: 'Parallel run variance' (legacy vs new totals, narrated)

Risks. Every money read re-points, so add before/after figure parity tests. This evolves BillingCase; it is not a second ledger. The seeded billing history and PERSIST_VERSION change.

What to check when it's done
  • ledgerParity.test.ts, written before any code change and keyed on invoice numbers and Booking ids, still passes with only import renames. BillingCase is gone, not wrapped, and the item 8 grep gates are clean (the only Xero reads left are MasterData's archiving view and the demo-trigger picker).
  • Reset shows the Ledger 'In balance'. 'Inject unmatched receipt' on /admin/ledger takes it out of balance by $120.00 with one check and a nav badge. 'Allocate to invoice' or 'Mark refunded' brings it back. AA fee money and the prepaid excess never move the imbalance.
  • A handoff fault leaves a complete pair marked 'Not yet in Xero', still visible to the anaesthetist the next day. The Billing monitor's own Run payables button pays from payable legs only. The PWA 'Office runs payables' stand-in is badged and moves Balances' Paid out.
  • npm run build, npm run build:pwa, npx vitest run and npm run shots are green. PERSIST_VERSION is bumped by one. The S3 Beat 5 'The ledger balances' and the mirror-wording fixes are in the demo guide and master guide.

Xero mirror resilience

Money After phase 36 1 session plan doc ↗ 3 items covered

The Xero mirror now behaves like a real integration that can fail, and the Phase 36 ledger stays the system of record. An ACCPAY is paid in Xero's payables run, and the engine records the disbursement against the payable leg only when a bill-paid webhook or the poll reports it, dated at the bill payment. The Billing monitor's own "Run payables in Xero" button stays as the office view. In a simulated outage, every engine-to-Xero call waits in a visible queue with an attempt count and backoff on the demo clock. Authorising still works, and restoring Xero drains the queue with no duplicates. The armed handoff fault becomes one queued failure that retries by itself. An invoice voided or amended directly in Xero is flagged at the next poll, and the office records a provisional resolution that moves no money. A bulk St George's remittance pays two invoices through the normal webhook and leaves one unmatched line in a Xero-side Bank reconciliation tab. A source-scan test keeps remittance matching out of the engine.

Prepayment letters, credits and refunds

Prepayment After phases 27, 39 2 sessions plan doc ↗ 8 items covered

Finish the prepayment story, keeping it thin and labelled provisional (almost everything is Proposed or Open: OQ-03, OQ-40, OQ-42). Plain-text letter templates with merge fields (patient, procedure, estimate, anaesthetist) and a fixed estimate notice that cannot be removed. Admins maintain them in Master data. The raise flow gets a picker and a preview, and each letter is snapshotted on the prepayment invoice. 'Send pre-payment reminder' goes on the Booking. When the prepayment is more than the final fee, the run settles it at authorise with a partial credit through Phase 39's single credit path (a wider cause and a settlement amount), linked to the prepayment invoice. Refunds reuse 39's held-credit and recovery totals in the ledger. A derived trust account view shows prepayments received, amounts paid on to anaesthetists, refunds due and paid, and any shortfall. Cancelling a prepaid Booking offers 'Refund pre-payment from trust account': a full credit against every prepayment invoice, and a refund of what was received, recorded against whichever anaesthetist held the prepayment. The payout is recorded, then paid by the bank. Rebooking with another anaesthetist goes through createBooking and raises a fresh prepayment at their own unit value, with both estimates visible.

Demo triggers and risks

Then enter plan mode: turn the work items into steps, name the exact files and tests each step touches, and wait for my approval. The outline says one session; realistically this is two. Plan session 1 as work items 1 to 9 (full-scale data, paused persistence, timings and the paged screens), stopped green, and session 2 as items 10 to 15, the trigger tests, the demo guide and the review pass; do both in one only if session 1 runs well.

While working:
- The full-scale dataset is built in memory, deterministically, from slotRng streams off SEED and the demo clock, on top of the pristine seed: the 14 demo anaesthetists and every seeded record stay exactly as they are, and 71 synthetic anaesthetists carry the volume (85 in all, about 20,000 Slots in the four-month horizon, about 28,000 invoices in the last 12 months). Every figure lives in one FULL_SCALE constants block or is counted from the data.
- The generator lives in src/domain/seed/fullScale/ with its own index and is NOT re-exported from domain/seed/index.ts (that would defeat the dynamic import). domainPurity.test.ts forbids it importing the store, so lift anything it must share with the runtime into src/domain first with a thin store wrapper: at least the archive rule (eligibleArchiveContactIds, rewritten linear by grouping ACCRECs per contact; today it is quadratic at scale) and any store selector the billing run leans on. Export FIRST_NAMES and SURNAMES from domain/seed/patients.ts. Leave the 71's last two working days of worked Lists SUBMITTED, so the Review queue shows a practice-sized backlog.
- Generated history goes through the same pure builders the billing run and the ledger use (locked record, invoices, ledger pairs, Xero mirror), never through store actions, and every ledger pair balances. Ids come from the counters, generated invoice numbers use a reserved series, and the next runtime id is unused.
- No Date.now(), new Date() or Math.random() in the data path. performance.now() is for measurement only, lives only in src/store/scaleMode.ts and src/shared/scale/ (everything else, including the leak scan, measures through scaleMode's timed() helper), and never enters AppState.
- While full-scale data is loaded, saving pauses and nothing is serialised (wrap the persist storage so setItem returns before createJSONStorage stringifies). Reset resumes saving before it installs the pristine seed; a reload after loading returns the pristine seed; the pause is separate from the storage-error latch. The scale mode and timings live in a non-persisted store.
- loadFullScaleData returns an Outcome at once (the registry's run is synchronous) and does the work asynchronously, reporting progress, result and any error through the scaleMode store and the banner; the frame wait is an injected scheduler so Vitest does not rely on requestAnimationFrame.
- Admin Day pages at 30 anaesthetists (no pager at demo scale; the narrated paging sentence goes), Invoices at 50 with a search, Audit at 100 (the 500-row cap goes), and the Ledger screen (and Phase 40's Admin Patients list, if it renders every patient) pages if it lists every row, all through one new apps/admin/components/Pager.tsx (tableChrome.ts stays a style-helper .ts). The Review queue is timed and indexed, not paged. The archive job and the reconciliation poll stay fast on Next day at full scale. Filters and search apply before paging. Fix hot selectors with identity-keyed memoised indexes only where the timings show a need.
- The working view on the matching screen shows only the mapped fields. The raw row is held on ImportRow, never edited, and shown only through viewRawImportRow: office only, reason required, one audit entry with the reason and never a value, payload masked by the provisional RAW_ROW_MASKING policy. A non-office attempt is refused and audited. Bump PERSIST_VERSION once for the ImportRow shape.
- Diagnostics go through reportDiagnostic, which redacts NHI-shaped tokens; refuse() records refusals; no console call remains outside diagnostics.ts. The NHI leak scan tokenises each area once (Xero including the sync queue, the diagnostic log, failure reasons), flags known NHIs and any valid NHI-shaped token, reports the core platform as "held by design", never shows an unmasked NHI, and proves itself with the planted-leak choice.
- Every write goes through mutate() with an audit entry (the full-scale install and Reset are the only wholesale replaces, side by side in mutate.ts), and every new action code gets a label in ACTION_LABELS.
- Demo triggers go through the Phase 14 registry, shown only on their screen, surfaces bar, with disabled states and bodies in src/store or src/shared: "Load full-scale data" (with a confirm) on Admin Day and Invoices, "Scan for NHI leaks" on the Data Inspector, "View raw row as another role" on the matching screen for the selected unmatched row (SOUTER_ACTOR, and a new HOSPITAL_FEED_ACTOR with role system and source integration, since ActorRole has no integration role). Update the pinned per-screen counts in demoTriggers.test.ts. The office's own View raw row is a product action in the row panel. The generator is dynamically imported. Nothing is added to the Control Panel page. No PWA stand-in is needed; confirm pwaPurity.test.ts stays green.
- Follow the design files: teal is the only action colour, crimson stays identity only, the Synthetic data only badge is neutral (not the demo warning tint), the scale banner is harness chrome in the warning tint, scan results use semantic tints (never the schedule status colours), and counts, ids, timings and masked values are mono with tabular-nums. No en or em dashes in any app copy.
- Keep npm run build, npm run build:pwa and npx vitest run green throughout.

When done:

Take every figure and label from the running app.
- Do not commit. Give me short, clear notes on what changed.

Phase goal: a presenter can load the whole practice (85 anaesthetists, about 20,000 Slots, about 28,000 invoices) in memory and see Admin Day, Invoices and Audit page at that scale with measured timings, and can show the NHI policy working: a restricted, audited, masked raw hospital row, a leak scan that finds no NHI outside the core platform, and a Synthetic data only badge on every app.

Milestones

What a workshop can be shown once each milestone phase is done.

  • After phase 16
    Booking vocabulary everywhere. Every demo action on its own screen, in the harness bar and on a handset, with the HL7/FHIR tooling badged Future scope. The corrected payables story: the payable equals the receivable, and the AA fee is its own invoice.
  • After phase 25
    The catalogue's pricing model end to end: editable RVG, modifier and Procedure masters feeding base units, one filtered Contract per Procedure with a default, billable party and required inputs on the Booking, invoices sent by the billing run, the 3/2/2 modifier split, a Contract-gated adjustment, and a lock at AUTHORISED that reproduces invoices exactly. S3, S4 Beat 3 and S5 are re-scripted.
  • After phase 27
    Prepayment from the anaesthetist's prepaid set: estimate, invoice at setup, part-paid tracking, the date-driven alert and the balance invoice. S4 Beat 1 is re-scripted.
  • After phase 32
    The office's schedule: Slots and Lists, the availability calendar, the conflict dashboard, Draft Lists with a waiting flag, blacklist warnings, and swaps with office confirmation. S2 is re-scripted.
  • After phase 35
    S1 rebuilt: hospital sync, then the matching screen, then a Booking on its default Contract. Explicit save and the update email.
  • After phase 39
    The money story on the internal ledger: balances shown in or out of balance, Xero mirror resilience, ledger-backed web accounts, additional invoices, and credit-and-rebill. S4 Beats 2 to 5 are re-scripted.
  • After phase 44
    Every verified gap closed: patients and alerts, prepayment letters, credits and trust refunds, reference-data loads, full-scale and privacy demos, and the rewritten S1 to S5 run sheet.
